oik_refresh_d_apostolic_church = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_game_rule = oik_special_names_interface }
		set_title_name = d_apostolic_church
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_game_rule = oik_special_names_foreign_rule }
		if = {
			limit = {
				holder ?= {
					top_liege != this
					top_liege = { culture = { has_name_list = name_list_anglo_saxon } }
					culture = { NOT = { has_name_list = name_list_anglo_saxon } }
				}
			}
			set_title_name = cn_oik_anglo_saxon_d_apostolic_church
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				holder ?= {
					top_liege != this
					top_liege = { culture = { has_name_list = name_list_castilian } }
					culture = { NOT = { has_name_list = name_list_castilian } }
				}
			}
			set_title_name = cn_oik_castilian_d_apostolic_church
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				holder ?= {
					top_liege != this
					top_liege = { culture = { has_name_list = name_list_english } }
					culture = { NOT = { has_name_list = name_list_english } }
				}
			}
			set_title_name = cn_oik_english_d_apostolic_church
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				holder ?= {
					top_liege != this
					top_liege = { culture = { has_name_list = name_list_french } }
					culture = { NOT = { has_name_list = name_list_french } }
				}
			}
			set_title_name = cn_oik_french_d_apostolic_church
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				holder ?= {
					top_liege != this
					top_liege = { culture = { has_name_list = name_list_greek } }
					culture = { NOT = { has_name_list = name_list_greek } }
				}
			}
			set_title_name = cn_oik_greek_d_apostolic_church
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				holder ?= {
					top_liege != this
					top_liege = { culture = { has_name_list = name_list_han } }
					culture = { NOT = { has_name_list = name_list_han } }
				}
			}
			set_title_name = cn_oik_han_d_apostolic_church
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				holder ?= {
					top_liege != this
					top_liege = { culture = { has_name_list = name_list_mongol } }
					culture = { NOT = { has_name_list = name_list_mongol } }
				}
			}
			set_title_name = cn_oik_mongol_d_apostolic_church
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				holder ?= {
					top_liege != this
					top_liege = { culture = { has_name_list = name_list_norman } }
					culture = { NOT = { has_name_list = name_list_norman } }
				}
			}
			set_title_name = cn_oik_norman_d_apostolic_church
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				holder ?= {
					top_liege != this
					top_liege = { culture = { has_name_list = name_list_norse } }
					culture = { NOT = { has_name_list = name_list_norse } }
				}
			}
			set_title_name = cn_oik_norse_d_apostolic_church
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				holder ?= {
					top_liege != this
					top_liege = { culture = { has_name_list = name_list_persian } }
					culture = { NOT = { has_name_list = name_list_persian } }
				}
			}
			set_title_name = cn_oik_persian_d_apostolic_church
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				holder ?= {
					top_liege != this
					top_liege = { culture = { has_name_list = name_list_roman } }
					culture = { NOT = { has_name_list = name_list_roman } }
				}
			}
			set_title_name = cn_oik_roman_d_apostolic_church
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				holder ?= {
					top_liege != this
					top_liege = { culture = { has_name_list = name_list_italian } }
					culture = { NOT = { has_name_list = name_list_italian } }
				}
			}
			set_title_name = cn_oik_italian_d_apostolic_church
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				holder ?= {
					top_liege != this
					top_liege = { culture = { has_name_list = name_list_cisalpine } }
					culture = { NOT = { has_name_list = name_list_cisalpine } }
				}
			}
			set_title_name = cn_oik_cisalpine_d_apostolic_church
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				holder ?= {
					top_liege != this
					top_liege = { culture = { has_name_list = name_list_lombard } }
					culture = { NOT = { has_name_list = name_list_lombard } }
				}
			}
			set_title_name = cn_oik_lombard_d_apostolic_church
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				holder ?= {
					top_liege != this
					top_liege = { culture = { has_name_list = name_list_sicilian } }
					culture = { NOT = { has_name_list = name_list_sicilian } }
				}
			}
			set_title_name = cn_oik_sicilian_d_apostolic_church
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				holder ?= {
					top_liege != this
					top_liege = { culture = { has_name_list = name_list_sardinian } }
					culture = { NOT = { has_name_list = name_list_sardinian } }
				}
			}
			set_title_name = cn_oik_sardinian_d_apostolic_church
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				holder ?= {
					top_liege != this
					top_liege = { culture = { has_name_list = name_list_vlach } }
					culture = { NOT = { has_name_list = name_list_vlach } }
				}
			}
			set_title_name = cn_oik_vlach_d_apostolic_church
		}
		else = {
			set_title_name = d_apostolic_church
		}
	}
	else = {
		reset_title_name = yes
	}
}

			set_title_name = cn_oik_sardinian_d_zun_warriors
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				holder ?= {
					top_liege != this
					top_liege = { culture = { has_name_list = name_list_vlach } }
					culture = { NOT = { has_name_list = name_list_vlach } }
				}
			}
			set_title_name = cn_oik_vlach_d_zun_warriors
		}
		else = {
			set_title_name = d_zun_warriors
		}
	}
	else = {
		reset_title_name = yes
	}
}

			}
			set_title_name = cn_oik_persian_k_papal_state
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				holder ?= {
					top_liege != this
					top_liege = { culture = { has_name_list = name_list_roman } }
					culture = { NOT = { has_name_list = name_list_roman } }
				}
			}
			set_title_name = cn_oik_roman_k_papal_state
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				holder ?= {
					top_liege != this
					top_liege = { culture = { has_name_list = name_list_italian } }
					culture = { NOT = { has_name_list = name_list_italian } }
				}
			}
			set_title_name = cn_oik_italian_k_papal_state
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				holder ?= {
					top_liege != this
					top_liege = { culture = { has_name_list = name_list_cisalpine } }
					culture = { NOT = { has_name_list = name_list_cisalpine } }
				}
			}
			set_title_name = cn_oik_cisalpine_k_papal_state
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				holder ?= {
					top_liege != this
					top_liege = { culture = { has_name_list = name_list_lombard } }
					culture = { NOT = { has_name_list = name_list_lombard } }
				}
			}
			set_title_name = cn_oik_lombard_k_papal_state
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				holder ?= {
					top_liege != this
					top_liege = { culture = { has_name_list = name_list_sicilian } }
					culture = { NOT = { has_name_list = name_list_sicilian } }
				}
			}
			set_title_name = cn_oik_sicilian_k_papal_state

		else_if = {
			limit = {
				holder ?= {
					top_liege != this

					culture = { NOT = { has_name_list = name_list_sardinian } }

					culture = { NOT = { has_name_list = name_list_vlach } }

			}
			set_title_name = cn_oik_vlach_k_papal_state
		}
		else = {
			set_title_name = k_papal_state
		}
	}
	else = {
		reset_title_name = yes
	}
}
